For $M=U\\Sigma V^\\top$, the idealized polar update $Q=UV^\\top$ gives every singular direction the same weight. We refer to this as the flat profile. Several recent optimizers replace this flat profile with fine-grained spectral maps that give each direction its own gain. We ask how much of this spectral detail a Muon update needs. Our spectral diagnostics show that approximately $94$--$97\\%$ of measured singular modes lie below an es","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-10-07","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2610_07497.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["stat.ML","cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"研究 Muon 优化器是否真的需要对不同奇异方向做细粒度 spectral shaping，还是较粗的频带级重加权已经足够。","importance":"贡献类型是优化器机制分析和训练 recipe 候选改进。Muon 的 polar update 对所有奇异方向给相同权重，而近期一些优化器尝试引入更细的谱映射；这篇论文直接检验这些谱细节是否必要，对大模型预训练优化器选择和 ablation 设计有参考价值。","technical_approach":"论文把 Muon 的矩阵 momentum 写成奇异值分解形式，分析 flat profile 下的 singular modes。提出 BulkBoost，两种版本分别是 fixed-rank 和 noise-calibrated。noise-calibrated 版本用 split-minibatch gradient differences 校准 Marchenko-Pastur 噪声边界，把边界以下的 bulk 和边界以上的 spike 分开，并用一个共享增益提高 bulk 相对权重，同时保持每个更新的 Frobenius norm。","experiments":"摘要报告 spectral diagnostics 中约 94%-97% 的 measured singular modes 位于估计噪声边界以下，但这些 bulk modes 合计仍与 reference gradient 正向对齐。论文比较 flat Muon、细粒度 spectral map 和 BulkBoost 变体；具体模型规模、任务、训练 token 和最终指标提升在提供的摘要中未列出。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks whether Muon really needs fine-grained spectral shaping across singular directions, or whether coarse band-level reweighting is enough.","importance":"The contribution is optimizer analysis plus a candidate training-recipe modification. Testing whether those spectral details matter is directly relevant to optimizer choices and ablation design for large-model pretraining.","technical_approach":"The paper analyzes Muon's matrix momentum through its SVD and the flat singular profile induced by the polar update. positional aliasing and lowers extrapolation-length conditional cross-entropy without hurting short-context loss."},"why_scales":{"zh":"RoPE 混叠来自确定性位置编码：当距离超过某个通道周期后，不同相对位置会在该通道上产生相同或相近相位。这个问题不依赖模型容量，放大模型只会让模型更擅长利用位置特征，但不能改变相位折叠本身。WavePrune 去掉的是错误归纳偏置，而不是增加参数或训练技巧；因此 100 倍算力下收益不应被简单抹平。可能的秩反转主要来自大模型通过数据和深层注意力学会绕开混叠，但从机制上看，干预作用在输入到 attention score 的几何结构上，跨尺度稳定性较高。","en":"RoPE aliasing is induced by the deterministic positional map: beyond the period of a frequency channel, different relative positions share similar phases. This failure mode is not removed by larger capacity; scale can learn to use positional features better, but it does not undo phase folding. WavePrune removes a bad positional bias rather than adding capacity or tuning. A larger model may partially route around the aliasing, but the intervention changes the geometry entering attention scores, so the effect should remain comparatively stable across scale."},"plan":{"goal":{"zh":"验证首周期 RoPE 截断是否在从头预训练和长上下文继续训练中都稳定改善长度外推，并检查短上下文是否受损。","en":"Test whether first-period RoPE truncation improves length extrapolation in both from-scratch pretraining and long-context continual training, while preserving short-context quality."},"data_sources":{"zh":"使用相同 tokenizer 和去重文本混合，训练 300M、700M、1.5B 三档 dense decoder-only 模型。主训练长度 4k，训练 token 按 Chinchilla-like 比例或固定 Iso-FLOPs ladder；另做 4k→32k 的 long-context continual pretrain 小阶段。","en":"Use the same tokenizer and deduplicated text mixture for 300M, 700M, and 1.5B dense decoder-only models. Another risk is that larger models or continual long-context training learn higher-layer compensation for RoPE aliasing, reducing from-scratch gains."},"source_paper_ids":["2610.06963"],"scaling_confidence":0.8},{"pillar":"arch_optimizer","title":{"zh":"Shampoo散度校正二阶低估","en":"Bregman-corrected Shampoo"},"claim":{"zh":"在有限窗口估计 Kronecker 二阶矩时，选择能补偿经验谱低估的 Bregman 散度，可在等算力下提升 Shampoo 预训练收敛，并减少 loss spike。","en":"When Kronecker second moments are estimated from finite windows, a Bregman divergence that compensates empirical spectral underestimation improves Shampoo pretraining convergence under equal compute and reduces loss spikes."},"why_scales":{"zh":"这里的关键不是某个小模型超参，而是梯度二阶矩的有限样本谱偏差。大模型层宽更大、矩阵梯度维度更高，若预条件统计窗口没有同比例扩大，经验二阶矩的尾部和小特征值低估不会自然消失。Bregman 散度改变的是预条件矩阵对谱误差的惩罚方式，相当于在优化器几何上加入稳定的偏置；只要梯度噪声和统计窗口比例相近，跨尺度相对排序应比普通学习率细调更稳定。","en":"The core effect is not a small-model hyperparameter but finite-sample spectral bias in gradient second-moment estimates. As model width grows, matrix-gradient dimension increases; unless the preconditioner statistics window scales proportionally, underestimation in the spectrum, especially the tail and small eigenvalues, does not disappear. The Bregman divergence changes how the preconditioner penalizes spectral error, adding a stable geometric bias to the optimizer. If gradient noise and statistics-window ratios are matched, its ranking should transfer better than ordinary LR tuning."},"plan":{"goal":{"zh":"验证不同 Bregman-Shampoo 变体的收益是否来自二阶矩谱估计校正，而不是额外算力、学习率或 batch 噪声差异。","en":"Verify whether gains from Bregman-Shampoo variants come from correcting second-moment spectral estimation rather than extra compute, LR tuning, or batch-noise differences."},"data_sources":{"zh":"使用 150M、350M、1B 三档 decoder-only LM，同一文本混合与 tokenizer。每档做 AdamW、标准 Shampoo、Frobenius-Shampoo、KL/Bregman-Shampoo 变体；总训练 FLOPs、token 数、global batch token、warmup 和 decay schedule 固定。","en":"Use 150M, 350M, and 1B decoder-only LMs with the same text mixture and tokenizer.
